# Devin automations port map

Planning only · 2026-10-12 · branch `plan-devin-port`

## 1. Authority and evidence

[RESTART §2](RESTART-2026-10-12.md#2-product-shape-v1-deliberately-small) governs: copy Devin's automations layout; port Cursor-only **features** into that layout. This document authorizes no implementation or PR. Inference remains Linear-only. Provider-trigger inventory is not authorization for other inference providers.

**Observed** means an existing capture report records the interaction. **Extracted** means source/spec/catalog evidence. **Port mapping** means a proposed adaptation of an identified existing component, not a claim that the combination shipped. **Unknown** is not permission to invent. No new visuals are specified.

The requested `~/devin-decompile` path does not exist here. The source used is `~/automation-project/devin-decompile`, pinned below. The requested `~/cursor-decompile/corpus/automations.split.js` and entire `corpus/` directory are absent; filename search under `/Users/irene` found no bundle. Therefore the Cursor comparison is bounded by `facts/`, not a completed direct-bundle audit. A complete “all Cursor-only features” claim remains blocked on that artifact. Archived implementations and their DESIGN behavior are not evidence or code donors.

`D` means the pinned Devin checkout; `S` its DECOMPILED_SPEC, `C` its COPY_CATALOG, `V` its FULL_ANNOTATED_DECOMPILE. S section numbers identify passages with underlying hashed-module/line citations. Appendix A cross-indexes component references and exact catalog copy, including descriptions, errors, plurals and dynamic copy families. Catalog availability alone does not prove a visible control.

### Layout and inventory qualifications

Copy the centered 800px editor, 12px horizontal/28px vertical padding, 24px section gaps, 10px card radii and sticky action bar (S §§8.1,19.2). Keep Devin's section order. List uses Name / Last 30 days / Last triggered, not Cursor's table. Gallery uses existing rounded cards and 2/3-column grid. Event charts are 166px high; issue sheet is 560px (S §§6.4,7.3,18).

The full captured inventory also includes Preflight enable/disclosure, runtime/environment/snapshot, timeout, script modal, secrets picker, contract text, clear persistent state confirmation, conflict handling, code-only save, and run/log modal (S §13.5, Appendix I §11). Those are **not implemented in the listed clean feature files**. The same applies to the complete scratchpad file browser/editor and original rich mention editor. `ScratchpadRow` exposes a callback, but the page does not supply it. Recover captured components before claiming these controls work. Appendix A retains their captured copy and call sites.

`ErrorLogsPage` is expressly reconstructed: its 50-row/90-day query, 960px frame and session precedence are not verified original behavior. Only its route and catalog copy are established. Quarantine it from exact-copy approval until the org-scoped original is captured; do not promote its chosen constants into the contract.

Other donor differences matter: local `model/sources.tsx` is a partial static schema registry, whereas captured schemas are dynamic; the editor's singleton set includes schedule/code scan, whereas the captured singleton is only incoming webhook; `model/rrule.ts` is a minimal parser that drops unsupported properties and interprets DTSTART using local time; the original validates timezone conversion. The local data store is mock transport, not the production API. See S Appendix I §§12–14 and Appendix A; these are repair requirements, not new product features.

Template filtering hides explicitly disconnected required integrations and unsupported triggers. Missing MCPs reduce readiness ordering; they do not hide templates. Stable ordering is unused, then ready, then server order. Search requires every token somewhere in combined searchable metadata. No catalog list can establish current server templates (S §§7.1–7.2).

## 4. Run-history and run-detail contract

### 4.1 Payloads and identity

**Extracted client projections**, not complete backend schemas (S Appendix A:1879–1912,2176–2251). Organization-scoped endpoints: `GET {org}/automations/{id}/events`, `/invocations`, `/issues`, `/consumption`, `/code-step/runs/{runId}`. Events/invocations return `{data: T[], total: number, has_next: boolean}`. Keep all five kinds of ID distinct.

```ts
type Event = {
  event_id: string;
  status: 'queued' | 'running' | 'succeeded' | 'failed' | 'skipped' | 'canceled' | string;
  created_at: string;
  event_type?: string | null; trigger_id?: string | null;
  event_message?: string | null; event_resource_url?: string | null;
  event_message_parts?: {text: string; resource_url?: string | null}[];
  error_message?: string | null;
  investigation_devin_id?: string | null; devin_session_id?: string | null;
  code_step_run_id?: string | null;
};
```

Invocations consumed by the issue sheet require `invocation_id`, `created_at`, optional message and both session references. Issues require `issue_id`, optional title/short_description, occurrence count, first/last invocation timestamps. Consumption returns `{data: {created_at: string, acu_used: number}[]}`.

```ts
type CodeStepRun = {
  code_step_run_id: string; status: 'succeeded' | 'failed' | string;
  created_at: string; wall_seconds: number | null;
  error_class: string | null; error_message: string | null; exit_code: number | null;
  envelope?: {kind: 'skip'; reason?: string} | {kind: 'items'; items?: unknown[]} | {kind: string};
  logs?: string | null;
};
```

The six event statuses are display cases, not a proven server transition graph. The UI proves neither queued→running→terminal ordering nor retry/exactly-once semantics. Unknown statuses must not be converted into success. Cursor's observed failed-launch record has `failureDetails.code=AUTOMATION_RUN_FAILURE_CODE_CLOUD_AGENT_PLAN_RESTRICTED`; that is separate from Devin `error_message`. Preserve the source failure information when adapting; do not copy Cursor billing requirements into Linear execution.

### 4.2 Query, rendering and refresh

| Consumer | Contract |
|---|---|
| Events | 50 rows, offset `page*50`, ISO since/until, repeated `status` params, `include_message=true`; query key includes org/id/all request dimensions |
| Activity | Same events endpoint, limit 5000, no messages; hourly buckets for at most eight days, otherwise daily; incomplete oldest bucket excluded when capped |
| Issues | Whole issue array, local inclusive range by first invocation; sort descending last invocation; deep-linked issue can be appended outside range |
| Issue invocations | `issue_id`, messages, limit 100; investigation records first, other server-ordered records under Grouped / Skipped |
| Consumption | ISO bounds; local daily aggregation; retain returned outside-range dates; stale time 30s |
| Refresh | Events poll 5s only while returned rows contain queued/running; Activity uses 30s under same predicate. Issues stale 30s, no proven timer; invocations no explicit poll. Terminal-only pages stop polling; this is not guaranteed detection of every new external event |
| Code run | Enabled with org/automation/run IDs; retry twice; poll 1.5s; stop on succeeded/failed, stale creation time, or uncached query error |

**Resolved spec contradiction:** S §18.4 and Appendix B say six minutes; Appendix A:2139 says six hours. V:7048–7086 contains `t - n >= 36e4`: **360,000ms, six minutes**. Invalid parsed creation time is also stale. Use verbatim source, not the six-hour prose.

Tabs: triage defaults Issues; ordinary automation Events; Consumption retains Events below its chart. Date defaults Last 4 weeks; Last week subtracts seven calendar days, Last 4 weeks subtracts 28, both through local end-of-today. Custom uses local start/end-of-day, no future dates, missing end=start. Preset left bound preserves time-of-day (S §18.1).

Status filter order: Queued, Running, Succeeded, Failed, Skipped, Canceled; multi-select badge and Clear filter. Date/status/raw search resets page. Search trims/lowercases and matches **only displayed message on current page**, not IDs/status/dates. Pagination uses server total, appears above 50; search reports matches on this page. Preserve cached rows on refetch error with inline Retry alert. No-cache errors replace rows; loading uses three 56px skeletons. Distinguish no events/range, status-empty, search-empty/page, and lifetime-empty Preflight states (S §18.3).

Message precedence: trimmed event message, synthesized schedule message, No message. Preserve resource links and first search-match highlighting. Session target is `investigation_devin_id ?? devin_session_id`, strip one leading `devin-`, then `/sessions/{id}`; no target is an em dash. Relative dates under seven days have exact UTC tooltip. Terminal error replaces status tooltip except succeeded/running/queued (S §18.3).

Activity maps succeeded→success, failed→failure, skipped/canceled→skipped, queued/running→in progress. Success rate is rounded `succeeded/(succeeded+failed)` or absent. Unknown statuses have no visible segment. Failed segments can link to error logs (S §18.2).

Preflight run link needs capability and a current-page run ID. Modal: Preflight run, badge, created time, optional rounded duration; skip reason, failure/class/exit code, emitted items and logs can coexist. Logs have byte/line counts, Copy/download and monospace body; revoke/recreate Blob URLs. Footer Close. Unfinished stale run says Stopped reporting. This is not a general session transcript viewer (S §18.4).

### 4.3 Manual run and mutations

Run now is disabled for incident actions, permission denial, disabled automation, frozen spending or disabled Pylon trigger. Dialog: idle→triggering→success; false/error→idle; close resets prompt/state/guard. Any recurring schedule hides context input; otherwise optional four-row textarea. `POST {org}/automations/{id}/trigger` sends `{prompt}` only for truthy prompt, otherwise **no body**. Success says Automation triggered / A new run will start shortly and appear in the events history. Acceptance is not completed execution (S §§17.3–17.4).

Success invalidates invocations/events/list/detail/sparklines/on-call statistics. Enabled toggle suppresses same-ID overlap, cancels queries, snapshots/optimistically patches caches, rolls back errors, then invalidates without clobbering another pending toggle (S Appendix A:2329–2360).

RESTART §3's log/checkpoint/delta architecture is future server work. It does not prove Devin streams deltas or uses websockets. Preserve visible refresh behavior; the adapter must supply these projections. Global Cursor scope and run details beyond the observed failure remain unknown.

## 5. Trigger and schedule editor contract

### 5.1 Trigger records and conditions

```ts
type Trigger = {
  event_type: string;
  conditions: {field: string; operator: string; value?: unknown}[][];
  replies?: {type: string; to?: {channel_id?: string}}[];
  trigger_id?: string;
};
```

Groups are OR alternatives of AND conditions. Schema queries are general `GET automations/schemas` and gated org-scoped `GET {org}/automations/schemas`; response projection is source→schema key→event_type/name/fields. Preserve server group/field order, required flags and types; available sources depend on connections/gates. Already-used sources remain representable. Org schema falls back to general while loading; missing data is not a license to manufacture fields (S Appendix I §12.1).

Known source labels: GitHub, GitLab, Slack, Linear, Jira, Pylon, incident.io, Schedule, Webhook, Security scan, Snapshot build. Runtime inventory is not exhaustively fixed by these labels. Add/type pickers use server event names except schedule virtual entries and Slack Watch channel. Only incoming webhook is a captured singleton. Restricted code-scan rows may lock type/removal; row replacement preserves stable identity (S Appendix I §§12.2–12.3).

Defaults: GitHub PR/issues action=opened; issue/PR-review comments=created; PR review=submitted; check run=completed; GitLab MR/issue=open; pipeline status=failed; code scan severity in critical/high. Pylon tag/status, Jira status, incident status/severity initialize required empty values. Other generic events have no conditions. Type changes reset defaults, filter incompatible replies, exit monitor mode and preserve incident team where applicable (S Appendix I §12.4).

Connection states distinguish Link user, Configure and Connect. Required fields get destructive rings after validation. Incident team is a special inline selector; org-wide serializes `incident.team_names is_empty null`. Slack monitor uses channel multi-select: zero→no condition, one→eq, many→in; queued channel joins precede save. Webhook bypasses generic fields: copy URL, secret Show/Hide or Regenerate, Copy, optional payload regex and test command. Reserved `_webhook_secret_hash` and `_webhook_body_regex` live in first group; never store the plaintext secret there (S Appendix I §§12.5,12.8–12.10).

Serialization removes empty event rows, empty field names/ordinary values/groups; retains `is_empty` and `_webhook_*`; trims globs; replies only with Start-session action. Reply defaults/conversion depend on source and Slack routing; do not treat replies as generic conditions. Create trims name/enables; edit preserves enabled and emits changed definitions/clear flags. Dirty equality omits webhook secret hash (S Appendix I §13).

### 5.2 Schedule presentation and serialization

All schedule picker variants persist as `event_type: 'schedule:recurring'` and `conditions: [[{field:'rrule', operator:'matches', value: rule}]]`. Editing replaces **all** schedule conditions with that one condition. Picker-only values hourly/daily/weekly/custom/one_time are not stored event types. Schedule display reads first group's first value; timezone defaults to browser zone (S Appendix I §§12.2,12.6).

| Choice | Existing Devin editor | Default |
|---|---|---|
| Every hour | Inline minute | Jittered minute |
| Every day | Inline hour/minute/timezone | 09:jittered minute |
| Every week | One weekday/hour/minute/timezone | Monday 09:jittered minute |
| Run once | Date/time popover, timezone, Apply | One hour from now |
| Custom schedule | 340px start-aligned Visual / RRULE popover | Empty rule |

Jitter minute is `[5,10,20,25,35,40,50,55][Math.floor(Math.random()*8)]`, held for mounted picker. Valid incoming minute wins. Weekly preset uses first BYDAY or Monday; multi-day weekly rules belong in Custom Visual. Read-only cards show summary without inputs (S Appendix I §§12.6–12.7).

Visual editing: interval >=1; Minute/Hour/Day/Week/Month; weekly seven circular pressed-state buttons, cannot remove last day; monthly day 1–31; hourly minute 0–59; daily/weekly/monthly hour 0–23 and minute 0–59; active timezone next to time. Switching tabs parses/serializes and clears error. Visual is disabled for unrepresentable raw rules. Raw is a three-row monospace textarea, placeholder `FREQ=WEEKLY;BYDAY=MO;BYHOUR=9;BYMINUTE=0`, help `RFC 5545 RRULE with an optional timezone.` Apply normalizes/validates; error keeps popover open, success emits/closes (S Appendix I §12.7).

Run once is DTSTART plus one-time RRULE, not a separate trigger type. Now refreshes every 30s; prohibit days before today in selected zone. Apply needs a strictly future instant. Saved stale-time warning has 60s grace. Calendar uses UTC coordinates with time enabled, no end date or own timezone display; adjacent timezone determines conversion. Apply writes RRULE, clears temporary range and closes. Do not copy the local helper's zone-naive `new Date(...)` as faithful behavior (S Appendix I §12.6; D `model/rrule.ts`).

Validator rejects empty rules, EXDATE/RDATE, malformed DTSTART, missing/unknown properties, SECONDLY, unsupported numeric ranges, one-time COUNT other than 1, COUNT with UNTIL. Plan restrictions can block sub-hourly schedules. Preserve original helper and error copy; do not replace it with the clean helper's permissive partial parser. RRULE examples are not a license to ignore unknown clauses. Scheduler ingestion/DST execution/missed runs/retries/dedup are remote unknowns (S Appendix I §12.7).

## 6. Verification and implementation handoff

This commit is documentation only. Verification consists of source/copy/link checks and `git diff --check`; no app/runtime tests or Computer-Use parity claim is made. No PR is opened. Future implementation still requires owner plan approval, tests, and live screenshots/interaction comparison under AGENTS.md.

Before implementation: restore Cursor bundle and complete direct gap audit; recover original error-log page; copy missing Preflight/scratchpad/rich-editor components; repair donor schema/singleton/RRULE deviations; capture connected provider/cycle fields; specify global-history and cron adapters from evidence. Keep model selection outside Linear-only v1. No blocked detail should be filled with invented copy, controls or payloads.

## Appendix B. Copy outside the Automations catalog

The captured Pylon profile confirmation imports other namespaces. These four rows are reproduced from S:5356–5363; they are not fabricated C keys. Dynamic schema labels and session-search helper copy likewise must be copied from their source rather than invented as catalog entries.

| Screen / state | Exact key | Exact English | Runtime behavior | Module evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Security profile / risky Pylon profile change / title | `pylon.securityWarningTitle` | <code>Warning</code> | Literal | `SecurityProfileSelect-B8zdT-I1.js:35`; `en.js:1942` |
| Security profile / risky Pylon profile change / body | `pylon.securityWarningDescription` | <code>Sessions created from Pylon process untrusted content like customer support tickets, which can attempt prompt injection. Make sure these sessions are resilient — apply a restrictive security profile that limits network and tool access.</code> | Literal | `SecurityProfileSelect-B8zdT-I1.js:38`; `en.js:1941` |
| Security profile / risky Pylon profile change / confirm | `pylon.securityWarningConfirmLabel` | <code>Change profile</code> | Literal | `SecurityProfileSelect-B8zdT-I1.js:39`; `en.js:1940` |
| Security profile / risky Pylon profile change / cancel | `confirmation.cancel` | <code>Cancel</code> | Literal | `SecurityProfileSelect-B8zdT-I1.js:40`; `en.js:740` |
